mwp_auto_supported_trigger = {
    is_ai = no
    is_alive = yes
    OR = {
        has_government = feudal_government
        has_government = republic_government
        has_government = clan_government
        has_government = tribal_government
        has_government = wanua_government
        has_government = mercenary_government
        has_government = holy_order_government
        has_government = monastic_holy_order_government
        has_government = administrative_government
        has_government = landless_adventurer_government
        has_government = nomad_government
        has_government = herder_government
        has_government = celestial_government
        has_government = mandala_government
        has_government = steppe_admin_government
        has_government = meritocratic_government
        has_government = japan_administrative_government
        has_government = japan_feudal_government
        has_government = theocracy_government
        has_government = ecclesiastical_government
    }
}
mwp_auto_fresh_trigger = {
    mwp_d20_war_schema_current_trigger = yes
    has_variable = mwp_scores_owner
    var:mwp_scores_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_scores_generation
    var:mwp_scores_generation = var:mwp_generation
}
mwp_analysis_current_work_trigger = {
    has_variable = mwp_generation
    OR = {
        AND = {
            has_variable = mwp_analysis_requested
            var:mwp_analysis_requested = yes
        }
        AND = {
            has_variable = mwp_scoring_active
            var:mwp_scoring_active = yes
            has_variable = mwp_scoring_owner
            var:mwp_scoring_owner = this
            has_variable = mwp_scoring_generation
            var:mwp_scoring_generation = var:mwp_generation
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_current_trigger = {
    mwp_d20_war_schema_current_trigger = yes
    has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_ready
    var:mwp_enemy_cache_ready = yes
    has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_owner
    var:mwp_enemy_cache_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_schema
    var:mwp_enemy_cache_schema = 1
    has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_epoch
    has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_day
    days_since_birth >= var:mwp_enemy_cache_day
    days_since_birth < mwp_enemy_cache_expiry_day_value
}
mwp_script_price_supported_trigger = {
    is_ai = no
    is_alive = yes
    OR = {
        has_government = feudal_government
        has_government = republic_government
        has_government = clan_government
        has_government = tribal_government
        has_government = wanua_government
        has_government = mercenary_government
        has_government = holy_order_government
        has_government = monastic_holy_order_government
        has_government = administrative_government
        has_government = landless_adventurer_government
        has_government = nomad_government
        has_government = herder_government
        has_government = celestial_government
        has_government = mandala_government
        has_government = steppe_admin_government
        has_government = meritocratic_government
        has_government = japan_administrative_government
        has_government = japan_feudal_government
        has_government = theocracy_government
        has_government = ecclesiastical_government
    }
    has_variable = mwp_script_price_collection_owner
    var:mwp_script_price_collection_owner = this
    mwp_d21_house_prices_current_trigger = yes
}
mwp_native_cost_current_trigger = {
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_ready
    var:mwp_native_cost_ready = yes
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_owner
    var:mwp_native_cost_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_n16c_owner
    var:mwp_n16c_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_n16c_clock
    has_variable = mwp_generation
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_generation
    var:mwp_native_cost_generation = var:mwp_generation
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_day
    var:mwp_native_cost_day = days_since_birth
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count
    var:mwp_native_cost_purchase_count = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_max_strength
    var:mwp_native_cost_max_strength = max_military_strength
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_clock_stamp
    mwp_n16c_quote_age_value >= 0
    mwp_n16c_quote_age_value <= 5
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_sequence
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_max_maa
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_maa
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_army
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_current_military
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_income
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_expenses
    has_variable = mwp_native_cost_raised_balance
    mwp_cost_cache_native_consistent_trigger = yes
}
mwp_fallback_cost_current_trigger = {
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_ready
    var:mwp_fallback_cost_ready = yes
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_owner
    var:mwp_fallback_cost_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_d16t_owner
    var:mwp_d16t_owner = this
    has_variable = mwp_d16t_clock
    has_variable = mwp_generation
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_generation
    var:mwp_fallback_cost_generation = var:mwp_generation
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_day
    days_since_birth = var:mwp_fallback_cost_day
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_purchase_count
    mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value = var:mwp_fallback_cost_purchase_count
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_max_strength
    max_military_strength = var:mwp_fallback_cost_max_strength
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_receipt_tick
    mwp_d16t_quote_age_value >= 0
    mwp_d16t_quote_age_value <= 5
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_income
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_expenses
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_current_military
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_levies
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_max_maa
    has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_sequence
}
mwp_cost_cache_native_consistent_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_native_cost_max_maa
            has_variable = mwp_native_cost_maa
        }
        mwp_cost_cache_actual_capacity_value = var:mwp_native_cost_max_maa
        mwp_cost_cache_regiment_count_value = maa_regiments_count
        monthly_character_men_at_arms_expense_gold >= 0
        OR = {
            AND = {
                maa_regiments_count = 0
                monthly_character_men_at_arms_expense_gold = 0
                var:mwp_native_cost_maa = 0
            }
            AND = {
                mwp_cost_cache_minimum_ratio_value > 0
                mwp_cost_cache_minimum_ratio_value = mwp_cost_cache_maximum_ratio_value
                mwp_cost_cache_native_error_value >= mwp_cost_cache_negative_rounding_value
                mwp_cost_cache_native_error_value <= mwp_cost_cache_rounding_value
            }
        }
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
}
mwp_cost_cache_fallback_consistent_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_unraised
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_ratio
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_known_count
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_zero_low_bound
            has_variable = mwp_cost_cache_fallback_capacity
        }
        monthly_character_men_at_arms_expense_gold = var:mwp_script_budget_unraised
        mwp_cost_cache_actual_capacity_value = var:mwp_cost_cache_fallback_capacity
        mwp_cost_cache_bound_ratio_value = var:mwp_script_budget_ratio
        mwp_cost_cache_known_count_value = var:mwp_script_budget_known_count
        mwp_cost_cache_zero_low_value = var:mwp_script_budget_zero_low_bound
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
}
mwp_cost_cache_fallback_display_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_ready
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_native
            has_variable = mwp_script_maa_upkeep
            has_variable = mwp_script_raised_army_upkeep
            has_variable = mwp_cost_cache_fallback_sequence
            has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_sequence
        }
        var:mwp_script_budget_ready = yes
        var:mwp_script_budget_native = 0
        mwp_cost_cache_fallback_consistent_trigger = yes
        mwp_fallback_cost_current_trigger = yes
        var:mwp_cost_cache_fallback_sequence = var:mwp_fallback_cost_sequence
        NOT = {
            mwp_native_cost_current_trigger = yes
        }
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
}
mwp_d18_army_snapshot_current_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_d18_army_factor
            has_variable = mwp_d18_army_exact
        }
        mwp_d18_army_factor_value = var:mwp_d18_army_factor
        mwp_d18_army_exact_value = var:mwp_d18_army_exact
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
}
mwp_d18_army_display_current_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_ready
            has_variable = mwp_script_budget_native
            has_variable = mwp_script_maa_upkeep
            has_variable = mwp_script_raised_army_upkeep
            has_variable = mwp_cost_cache_fallback_sequence
            has_variable = mwp_fallback_cost_sequence
        }
        var:mwp_script_budget_ready = yes
        mwp_d18_army_snapshot_current_trigger = yes
        mwp_fallback_cost_current_trigger = yes
        var:mwp_cost_cache_fallback_sequence = var:mwp_fallback_cost_sequence
        OR = {
            AND = {
                var:mwp_script_budget_native = 1
                mwp_native_cost_current_trigger = yes
            }
            AND = {
                var:mwp_script_budget_native = 0
                mwp_cost_cache_fallback_consistent_trigger = yes
            }
        }
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
    mwp_d19_whole_army_supported_trigger = yes
}

mwp_d19_transaction_paid_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_auto_last_paid_verified
            exists = var:mwp_auto_last_regiment
        }
        var:mwp_auto_last_paid_verified = yes
        var:mwp_auto_purchase_count = var:mwp_d19_tx_expected_counter
        mwp_d21_selected_wallet_value = mwp_d21_transaction_expected_wallet_value
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
}
mwp_d19_transaction_target_trigger = {
    mwp_d21_selected_title_capacity_trigger = yes
    mwp_d21_selected_route_valid_trigger = yes
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_script_selected_action = 1
        }
        always = yes
    }
    trigger_else = {
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                exists = var:mwp_script_selected_regiment
            }
            var:mwp_script_selected_action = 2
            trigger_if = {
                limit = {
                    mwp_d21_active_route_value = 2
                }
                var:mwp_script_selected_title = {
                    any_owned_title_maa_regiment = {
                        this = root.var:mwp_script_selected_regiment
                    }
                }
            }
            trigger_else = {
                any_maa_regiment = {
                    this = root.var:mwp_script_selected_regiment
                }
            }
            var:mwp_script_selected_regiment = {
                is_regular_maa_regiment = yes
                mwp_d21_selected_regiment_route_trigger = yes
                regiment_owner = {
                    this = root
                }
                maa_size > 0
                maa_max_troops_count > 0
            }
        }
        trigger_else = {
            always = no
        }
    }
}
mwp_d19_transaction_post_matches_trigger = {
    mwp_d21_selected_route_valid_trigger = yes
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_active
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_paid
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_owner
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_generation
            has_variable = mwp_generation
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_day
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_mode
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_counter
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_expected_counter
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_created_current
            has_variable = mwp_auto_purchase_count
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_gold
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_price
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_id
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_action
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_count
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_post_size
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_post_max
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_post_current
            exists = var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment
        }
        var:mwp_d19_tx_owner = this
        var:mwp_d19_tx_generation = var:mwp_generation
        var:mwp_d19_tx_day = days_since_birth
        var:mwp_d19_tx_mode = mwp_auto_budget_mode_value
        var:mwp_auto_purchase_count = var:mwp_d19_tx_expected_counter
        mwp_d21_selected_wallet_value = mwp_d21_transaction_expected_wallet_value
        var:mwp_d19_tx_paid = yes
        NOT = {
            has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_refunded
        }
        var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = {
            is_regular_maa_regiment = yes
            mwp_d21_selected_regiment_route_trigger = yes
            regiment_owner = {
                this = root
            }
            mwp_d19_transaction_type_trigger = yes
            maa_size = root.var:mwp_d19_tx_post_size
            maa_max_troops_count = root.var:mwp_d19_tx_post_max
            maa_current_troops_count = root.var:mwp_d19_tx_post_current
        }
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                var:mwp_d19_tx_action = 1
            }
            mwp_d21_route_count_value = mwp_d21_transaction_created_count_value
            var:mwp_d19_tx_post_size = 1
            var:mwp_d19_tx_post_max > 0
            var:mwp_d19_tx_post_current = var:mwp_d19_tx_created_current
            NOT = {
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_post_station
            }
        }
        trigger_else = {
            trigger_if = {
                limit = {
                    has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_size
                    has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_max
                    has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_current
                    has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_expected_size
                    exists = var:mwp_d19_tx_pre_regiment
                }
                var:mwp_d19_tx_action = 2
                mwp_d21_route_count_value = var:mwp_d19_tx_count
                var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = var:mwp_d19_tx_pre_regiment
                var:mwp_d19_tx_post_size = var:mwp_d19_tx_expected_size
                var:mwp_d19_tx_post_current = mwp_d21_nomad_expected_current_value
                var:mwp_d19_tx_post_max >= var:mwp_d19_tx_max
            }
            trigger_else = {
                always = no
            }
        }
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_post_station
            }
            var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = {
                exists = regiment_station
                regiment_station = root.var:mwp_d19_tx_post_station
            }
        }
        trigger_else = {
            var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = {
                NOT = {
                    exists = regiment_station
                }
            }
        }
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                var:mwp_d19_tx_action = 2
            }
            trigger_if = {
                limit = {
                    has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_station
                }
                var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = {
                    exists = regiment_station
                    regiment_station = root.var:mwp_d19_tx_station
                }
            }
            trigger_else = {
                var:mwp_d19_tx_regiment = {
                    NOT = {
                        exists = regiment_station
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    trigger_else = {
        always = no
    }
    mwp_d21_nomad_post_herd_trigger = yes
}

mwp_d19_post_purchase_safe_trigger = {
    trigger_if = {
        limit = {
            mwp_auto_budget_mode_value = 3
        }
        always = yes
    }
    trigger_else = {
        trigger_if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_ready
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_owner
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_generation
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_day
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_mode
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_gross
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_income
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_expense_limit
                has_variable = mwp_d19_net_purchase_count
                has_variable = mwp_generation
                has_variable = mwp_batch_active
                has_variable = mwp_batch_generation
                has_variable = mwp_batch_mode
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_active
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_owner
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_generation
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_day
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_mode
                has_variable = mwp_d19_tx_counter
            }
            var:mwp_d19_net_ready = yes
            var:mwp_d19_net_owner = this
            var:mwp_d19_net_generation = var:mwp_generation
            var:mwp_d19_net_day = days_since_birth
            var:mwp_d19_net_mode = mwp_auto_budget_mode_value
            var:mwp_d19_net_income <= var:mwp_d19_net_gross
            var:mwp_batch_active = yes
            var:mwp_batch_generation = var:mwp_d19_net_generation
            var:mwp_batch_mode = var:mwp_d19_net_mode
            var:mwp_d19_tx_active = yes
            var:mwp_d19_tx_owner = this
            var:mwp_d19_tx_generation = var:mwp_d19_net_generation
            var:mwp_d19_tx_day = var:mwp_d19_net_day
            var:mwp_d19_tx_mode = var:mwp_d19_net_mode
            var:mwp_d19_tx_counter >= var:mwp_d19_net_purchase_count
            monthly_character_expenses >= 0
            monthly_character_expenses <= {
                value = var:mwp_d19_net_income
                subtract = 0.01
            }
            monthly_character_expenses <= var:mwp_d19_net_expense_limit
        }
        trigger_else = {
            always = no
        }
    }
}

mwp_d19_forecast_regiment_supported_trigger = {
    is_personal_maa_regiment = yes
    is_regular_maa_regiment = yes
    is_title_maa_regiment = no
    is_event_maa_regiment = no
    is_hired_maa_regiment = no
    mwp_cost_cache_regiment_bound_ratio_value >= 0
}
mwp_d19_whole_army_supported_trigger = {
    NOT = {
        any_hired_mercenary = {
            always = yes
        }
    }
    NOT = {
        any_maa_regiment = {
            regiment_owner = root
            NOT = {
                mwp_d19_forecast_regiment_supported_trigger = yes
            }
        }
    }
    NOT = {
        any_army = {
            army_owner = root
            any_army_maa_regiment = {
                NOT = {
                    mwp_d19_forecast_regiment_supported_trigger = yes
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

mwp_d21_house_prices_current_trigger = {
 trigger_if = { limit = { NOT = { any_powerful_family = { always = yes } } } always = yes }
 trigger_else = {
is_ai = no is_alive = yes has_variable = mwp_d21r_owner var:mwp_d21r_owner = this has_variable = mwp_d21r_clock has_variable = mwp_d21r_epoch
 trigger_if = { limit = {
  has_variable = mwp_generation has_variable = mwp_d21h_epoch
  has_variable = mwp_d21h_generation has_variable = mwp_d21h_day
  has_variable = mwp_d21h_start
 }
 var:mwp_d21h_epoch = var:mwp_d21r_epoch
 var:mwp_d21h_generation = var:mwp_generation
 var:mwp_d21h_day = days_since_birth
 var:mwp_d21r_clock >= var:mwp_d21h_start
 var:mwp_d21r_clock <= { value = var:mwp_d21h_start add = 10 }
 }
 trigger_else = { always = no }

has_variable = mwp_d21h_ready var:mwp_d21h_ready = yes var:mwp_d21h_received = var:mwp_d21h_expected
}
}
